- **llm-fallback**: Owns the optional LLM fallback layer for unrecognized input: hard timeout, keyless graceful degradation, prompt construction with untrusted input isolation, and handoff of the model's output to the guardrail layer. Triggers on "fallback", "llm", "model call", "api key", "timeout", "unrecognized question", "clarification". &lt;example&gt; Context: The fast path returns unknown for a question and the developer wants the fallback exercised user: "what happens to a question the router cannot classify when there is no API key?" assistant: "I will use the llm-fallback agent to review the keyless degradat… ([nikita-voloshyn/store-voice-agent](https://github.com/nikita-voloshyn/store-voice-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/llm-fallback.md))

- **llm-feature-engineer**: Adviser on LLM-backed product features — RAG and Q&A, extraction, summarization, prompts built in code. Covers hallucination prevention, validating output against the source, prompt-injection defence, decisions in code not the model, and evals. Use when designing or reviewing an LLM feature, its prompt, parser or guardrails, or when it "makes things up". Not for Claude Code skills, agents or CLAUDE.md (edit-prompt skill) or generic backend design (backend-engineer). ([livemonkey1300/shell-hero](https://github.com/livemonkey1300/shell-hero/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/llm-feature-engineer.md))
- **llm-feature-test-engineer**: Designs and implements eval suites, runtime guardrail tests and adversarial prompt-injection suites for LLM-powered features, then wires the regression gate into CI. Use when a product ships prompts, agents or RAG and its quality is currently verified by eyeballing output, when a model or prompt upgrade needs a regression check, or when an AI feature is about to reach real users. The dispatch prompt carries a `PLAN: &lt;path&gt;` line naming a plan whose header names `executor: local-coder`. The agent mounts the plan as a small kit project outside the repository, runs the kit by following the `aider-setup` skill, brings back only the files the plan names, and reviews them under R35. Several plans or an unattended night go to the kit's `aider-night` directly. It never writes or edits code, never merges and never performs state-changing git (rules R38 and R39). ([LARi-UQAC/ResearchTools](https://github.com/LARi-UQAC/ResearchTools/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/local-coder.agent.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **local-codex-workers**: Every API or logging invocation selects its profile explicitly. Shell state does not persist between agent tool calls.
